```go
package lab

import (
	"os"
	"regexp"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"gopkg.in/yaml.v3"
)

func TestL73JunosLinesTypeTheDocumentedStatements(t *testing.T) {
	got := l73JunosLines(l73DeviceTLS{Server: "172.100.100.1"})
	want := []string{
		"delete system tacplus-server",
		"delete system accounting destination tacplus",
		"set system tacplus-server 172.100.100.1 routing-instance mgmt_junos",
		"set system tacplus-server 172.100.100.1 tls trusted-ca-group l73-trust",
		"set system tacplus-server 172.100.100.1 tls mutual-authentication certificate-id l73-dev",
		"set system accounting destination tacplus server 172.100.100.1 routing-instance mgmt_junos",
		"set system accounting destination tacplus server 172.100.100.1 tls trusted-ca-group l73-trust",
		"set system accounting destination tacplus server 172.100.100.1 tls mutual-authentication certificate-id l73-dev",
	}
	if !slices.Equal(got, want) {
		t.Fatalf("got\n%s\nwant\n%s", strings.Join(got, "\n"), strings.Join(want, "\n"))
	}
	got = l73JunosLines(l73DeviceTLS{Server: "172.100.100.1", Port: 300, SingleConnection: true,
		Second: "172.100.100.2", SecondTLS: false})
	for _, line := range []string{
		"set system tacplus-server 172.100.100.1 port 300",
		"set system tacplus-server 172.100.100.1 single-connection",
		"set system accounting destination tacplus server 172.100.100.1 port 300",
		"set system tacplus-server 172.100.100.2 routing-instance mgmt_junos",
		`set system tacplus-server 172.100.100.2 secret "labsecret"`,
	} {
		if !slices.Contains(got, line) {
			t.Errorf("missing %q", line)
		}
	}
	for _, line := range got {
		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "set system tacplus-server 172.100.100.2 tls") {
			t.Errorf("a plain second server carries tls: %q", line)
		}
		if strings.Contains(line, "172.100.100.1") && strings.Contains(line, "secret") {
			t.Errorf("a tls server carries a secret it was not asked for: %q", line)
		}
	}
}

func TestL73RestoreIsTheStartupConfigsServers(t *testing.T) {
	got := l73JunosRestore()
	want := []string{

	}
	if !slices.Equal(got, want) {

	}
}

// Coverage of the typed keywords by the asks is derived from the typed lines,
// not listed: tests/lab/l73_cover_unit_test.go (TestL73JunosAsksCoverEveryTypedKeyword,
// TestL73CiscoAsksCoverEveryTypedKeyword), added after Task 6a's review. What a help
// lists is recorded per ask and word (l73RecordListed, TestL73ListedIsKeyedByLevelAndWord).

func TestL73HelpVerdict(t *testing.T) {
	help := "Possible completions:\n  <[Enter]>            Execute this command\n" +
		"  port                 TACACS+ authentication server port number\n" +
		"  routing-instance     Routing instance\n  secret               Shared secret\n" +
		"  single-connection    Optimize TCP connection attempts\n  timeout              Request timeout period\n" +
		"  tls                  TLS configuration\n"
	miss, have, err := l73HelpVerdict(help, l73JunosAsks[0])
	if err != nil || len(miss) != 0 || len(have) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("%v %v %v", miss, have, err)
	}
	miss, _, err = l73HelpVerdict(strings.Replace(help, "  tls ", "  xyz ", 1), l73JunosAsks[0])
	if err != nil || !slices.Equal(miss, []string{"tls"}) {
		t.Fatalf("%v %v", miss, err)
	}
	if _, _, err := l73HelpVerdict("syntax error, expecting <command>.\n", l73JunosAsks[0]); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("an error read as an option list")
	}
	// Want words are reported when listed and never missed when not.
	ask := l73Ask{Ask: "x ", Control: []string{"client"}, Need: []string{"client"}, Want: []string{"server"}}
	miss, have, err = l73HelpVerdict("  client   Client trustpoint\n  server   Server trustpoint\n", ask)
	if err != nil || len(miss) != 0 || !slices.Equal(have, []string{"server"}) {
		t.Fatalf("%v %v %v", miss, have, err)
	}
	miss, have, err = l73HelpVerdict("  client   Client trustpoint\n", ask)
	if err != nil || len(miss) != 0 || len(have) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("%v %v %v", miss, have, err)
	}
}

func TestL73DaemonYAMLFromTheLabConfig(t *testing.T) {
	base, err := os.ReadFile("port49d-lab.yaml")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	out, err := l73DaemonYAML(string(base), l73Daemon{Inventory: "/x/inventory.yaml", Listen: "172.100.100.2:49",
		TLSListen: "172.100.100.1:300", Cert: "/x/s.crt", Key: "/x/s.key", ClientCA: "/x/root.pem",
		Intermediates: "/x/inter.pem", ServerNames: []string{"tacacs.l73.example", "a: b, c"}, Tickets: 10 * time.Minute,
		StateDir: "/x/state", Status: "{disabled: true}", FaultFile: "/x/fault", Attrs: "[]"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var cfg struct {
		Listen        string         `yaml:"listen"`
		InventoryFile string         `yaml:"inventory_file"`
		Limits        map[string]any `yaml:"limits"`
		Lab           map[string]any `yaml:"lab"`
		TLS           map[string]any `yaml:"tls_listener"`
	}
	if err := yaml.Unmarshal([]byte(out), &cfg); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("%v\n%s", err, out)
	}
	if cfg.Listen != "172.100.100.2:49" || cfg.InventoryFile != "/x/inventory.yaml" || cfg.Limits["malformed_backoff_base"] != "2s" ||
		cfg.Lab["fault_file"] != "/x/fault" || cfg.TLS["listen"] != "172.100.100.1:300" ||
		cfg.TLS["intermediates_file"] != "/x/inter.pem" || cfg.TLS["session_ticket_lifetime"] != "10m0s" {
		t.Fatalf("%+v", cfg)
	}
	if sn, ok := cfg.TLS["server_names"].([]any); !ok || !slices.Equal(sn, []any{"tacacs.l73.example", "a: b, c"}) {
		t.Fatalf("server_names %v", cfg.TLS["server_names"])
	}
	if sa, ok := cfg.Lab["session_attributes"].([]any); !ok || len(sa) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("session_attributes %v", cfg.Lab["session_attributes"])
	}
	out, err = l73DaemonYAML(string(base), l73Daemon{Inventory: "/i", TLSListen: "172.100.100.1:300", Cert: "c", Key: "k",
		ClientCA: "a", StateDir: "s", Status: "{disabled: true}"})
	if err != nil || !strings.Contains(out, `listen: "172.100.100.1:49"`) || strings.Contains(out, "intermediates_file") ||
		strings.Contains(out, "server_names") || strings.Contains(out, "fault_file") {
		t.Fatalf("%v\n%s", err, out)
	}
	// No Attrs keeps the lab file's session_attributes line as it is.
	var keep string
	for _, ln := range strings.Split(string(base), "\n") {
		if strings.HasPrefix(ln, "  session_attributes: ") {
			keep = ln
		}
	}
	if keep == "" || !strings.Contains(out, keep+"\n") {
		t.Fatalf("session_attributes changed with no Attrs:\n%s", out)
	}
	if _, err := l73DaemonYAML("listen: x\n", l73Daemon{Inventory: "/i"}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a config with no inventory_file line accepted")
	}
}

func TestL73InventoryMarksOneDeviceTLS(t *testing.T) {
	base, err := os.ReadFile("inventory-lab.yaml")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	out, err := l73Inventory(string(base), "srx1")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if strings.Count(out, "transport: tls") != 1 || !regexp.MustCompile(`\{name: srx1,.*transport: tls\}`).MatchString(out) {
		t.Fatal(out)
	}
	if _, err := l73Inventory(out, "srx1"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a device that already names a transport rewritten")
	}
	if _, err := l73Inventory(string(base), "nosuch"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("an absent device accepted")
	}
}

// Every L73 cell named in spec §15 has an entry, in its order, and nothing
// else does.
func TestL73CellsAreTheRegistersCells(t *testing.T) {
	spec, err := os.ReadFile("../../docs/design/2026-08-23-policy-engine-spec.md")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var row string
	for _, ln := range strings.Split(string(spec), "\n") {
		if strings.HasPrefix(ln, "| **L73** |") {
			row = strings.Split(ln, "|")[2]
		}
	}
	var want []string
	for _, m := range regexp.MustCompile(`\(([a-z])\)`).FindAllStringSubmatch(row, -1) {
		want = append(want, m[1])
	}
	var got []string
	for _, c := range l73Cells {
		got = append(got, c.ID)
	}
	if len(want) == 0 || !slices.Equal(got, want) {
		t.Fatalf("cells %v, the register's %v", got, want)
	}
	for _, c := range l73Cells {
		if !regexp.MustCompile(regexp.QuoteMeta("("+c.ID+") "+c.Claim) + `(;|\.|\s*$)`).MatchString(row) {
			t.Errorf("cell %s's claim is not the register's wording", c.ID)
		}
	}
}

func TestL73RenderTableNeedsEveryCellOnce(t *testing.T) {
	var obs []l73Obs
	for i := len(l73Cells) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
		obs = append(obs, l73Obs{ID: l73Cells[i].ID, Configured: "c|d", Observed: "o\nk", Docs: "n/a"})
	}
	table, err := l73RenderTable(obs)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(table, `c\|d`) || strings.Contains(table, "o\nk") || strings.Index(table, "| a |") > strings.Index(table, "| j |") {
		t.Fatal(table)
	}
	if _, err := l73RenderTable(obs[1:]); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a missing cell rendered")
	}
	if _, err := l73RenderTable(append(obs, obs[0])); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a cell twice rendered")
	}
	blank := slices.Clone(obs)
	blank[3].Observed = " "
	if _, err := l73RenderTable(blank); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a cell with nothing observed rendered as measured")
	}
}

func TestL73PEMBlockFromACLIScreen(t *testing.T) {
	screen := "file show /var/tmp/l73-dev.csr\r\n-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE REQUEST-----\r\n" +
		"MIIBDzCBtgIBADAPMQ0wCwYDVQQDDARzcngxMFkwEwYHKoZIzj0CAQYIKoZIzj0D\r\n" +
		"-----END CERTIFICATE REQUEST-----\r\n\r\nbreakglass@srx1> "
	b, err := l73PEMBlock(screen, "CERTIFICATE REQUEST")
	if err != nil || !strings.HasPrefix(string(b), "-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE REQUEST-----\n") || strings.Contains(string(b), "\r") ||
		!strings.HasSuffix(string(b), "-----END CERTIFICATE REQUEST-----\n") {
		t.Fatalf("%q %v", b, err)
	}
	if _, err := l73PEMBlock(screen, "CERTIFICATE"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a certificate request read as a certificate")
	}
	if _, err := l73PEMBlock("error: file not found\r\n> ", "CERTIFICATE REQUEST"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("no block read as one")
	}
}

func TestScpSinkFrame(t *testing.T) {
	got := string(scpSinkFrame("l73-root.pem", []byte("PEM\n")))
	if got != "C0644 4 l73-root.pem\nPEM\n\x00" {
		t.Fatalf("%q", got)
	}
}

func TestL73MetricValue(t *testing.T) {
	exp := "# HELP x\nport49_tls_handshakes_total{outcome=\"ok\"} 3\nport49_tls_handshakes_total{outcome=\"resumed\"} 2\n"
	if v, ok := l73MetricValue(exp, `port49_tls_handshakes_total{outcome="resumed"}`); !ok || v != 2 {
		t.Fatal(v, ok)
	}
	// An absent series is not a zero one: a misspelled selector must be seen.
	if v, ok := l73MetricValue(exp, `port49_tls_handshakes_total{outcome="unknown_ca"}`); ok || v != 0 {
		t.Fatal(v, ok)
	}
	if _, ok := l73MetricValue("port49_x_total 0\n", "port49_x_total"); !ok {
		t.Fatal("a series at zero read as absent")
	}
}

// A selector matches its own line only: not a longer name, and not a value
// glued on with no separator.
func TestL73MetricValueSelectorIsNotAPrefix(t *testing.T) {
	exp := "port49_x_total_extra{a=\"b\"} 9\nport49_x_totalfoo 8\nport49_x_total{a=\"b\"}7\nport49_x_total{a=\"b\"} 4\n"
	if v, ok := l73MetricValue(exp, `port49_x_total{a="b"}`); !ok || v != 4 {
		t.Fatal(v, ok)
	}
	if v, ok := l73MetricValue("port49_x_total{a=\"b\"}7\n", `port49_x_total{a="b"}`); ok || v != 0 {
		t.Fatal(v, ok)
	}
}

func TestL73ScrubLog(t *testing.T) {
	in := "Oct 17 tacplus: TLS handshake failed: bad certificate\n" +
		"Oct 17 secret \"$9$abc\"\nOct 17 token eyJhbGciOi\n" +
		"Oct 17 cert " + strings.Repeat("A1b2", 8) + " loaded\n"
	in += "Oct 17 authentication-key \"$9$ab\"\nOct 17 hash $6$short ok\n"
	got := l73ScrubLog(in)
	if strings.Contains(got, "$9$") || strings.Contains(got, "$6$") || !strings.Contains(got, "hash <HASH> ok") {
		t.Fatal(got)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(got, "TLS handshake failed: bad certificate") || strings.Contains(got, "$9$") ||
		strings.Contains(got, "eyJ") || strings.Contains(got, strings.Repeat("A1b2", 8)) || !strings.Contains(got, "<LONG>") {
		t.Fatal(got)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them and see them fail.** Run: `go test ./tests/lab/ -run '^(TestL73|TestScp)' -count=1`. Expected: `undefined: l73JunosLines` and the other names.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `tests/lab/l73_device.go`:
```go
package lab

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/pem"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"regexp"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/blanketfish/port49/tests/lab/labcreds"
)

// The Junos statements L73 types are the ones Juniper documents for 26.2R1
// ("TACACS+ over TLS overview": `tls trusted-ca-group` and
// `tls mutual-authentication certificate-id` under both `system
// tacplus-server <ip>` and `system accounting destination tacplus server
// <ip>`). The run's first cell asks the image's own help about every
// keyword here (l73JunosAsks) before any of them is committed.
const (
	l73Server       = "172.100.100.1" // the clab bridge's .1, where port49d listens
	l73SecondServer = "172.100.100.2" // splitSecondAddr's value (harness_test.go is lab-tagged)
	l73TLSPort      = 300
	l73Instance     = "mgmt_junos"
	l73TrustGroup   = "l73-trust"
	l73RootProfile  = "l73-root"
	l73InterProfile = "l73-inter"
	l73CertID       = "l73-dev"
	l73CSRPath      = "/var/tmp/l73-dev.csr"
)

// l73DeviceTLS is how the device's TACACS+ client is pointed at port49 in
// one cell.
type l73DeviceTLS struct {
	Server           string
	Port             int // 0: no port statement (cell a)
	SingleConnection bool
	Secret           bool // a shared secret beside tls, only if the image's commit demands one
	Second           string
	SecondTLS        bool
	SecondPort       int
}

func l73JunosLines(d l73DeviceTLS) []string {
	lines := []string{"delete system tacplus-server", "delete system accounting destination tacplus"}
	auth := "set system tacplus-server " + d.Server
	lines = append(lines, l73ServerLines(auth, true, d.Port, d.Secret)...)
	if d.SingleConnection {
		lines = append(lines, auth+" single-connection")
	}
	lines = append(lines, l73ServerLines("set system accounting destination tacplus server "+d.Server, true, d.Port, d.Secret)...)
	if d.Second != "" {
		lines = append(lines, l73ServerLines("set system tacplus-server "+d.Second, d.SecondTLS, d.SecondPort,
			d.Secret || !d.SecondTLS)...)
	}
	return lines
}

func l73ServerLines(prefix string, tls bool, port int, secret bool) []string {
	out := []string{prefix + " routing-instance " + l73Instance}
	if tls {
		out = append(out, prefix+" tls trusted-ca-group "+l73TrustGroup,
			prefix+" tls mutual-authentication certificate-id "+l73CertID)
	}
	if port != 0 {
		out = append(out, prefix+" port "+strconv.Itoa(port))
	}
	if secret {
		out = append(out, prefix+` secret "`+labcreds.Key+`"`)
	}
	return out
}

// l73JunosRestore is configs/vjunos-startup.conf's servers again.
func l73JunosRestore() []string {
	lines := []string{"delete system tacplus-server", "delete system accounting destination tacplus"}
	lines = append(lines, l73ServerLines("set system tacplus-server "+l73Server, false, 0, true)...)
	return append(lines, l73ServerLines("set system accounting destination tacplus server "+l73Server, false, 0, true)...)
}

// l73PKILines are the CA profiles and the trust group the device checks
// port49's certificate against. Revocation checking is off on the device:
// the lab's CAs publish no CRL, and whether Junos checks one is not an L73
// cell. The note says so.
func l73PKILines() []string {
	return []string{
		"set security pki ca-profile " + l73RootProfile + " ca-identity " + l73RootProfile,
		"set security pki ca-profile " + l73RootProfile + " revocation-check disable",
		"set security pki ca-profile " + l73InterProfile + " ca-identity " + l73InterProfile,
		"set security pki ca-profile " + l73InterProfile + " revocation-check disable",
	}
}

// l73TrustGroupLines is the trust group the device checks port49's
// certificate against. It is committed after the CA certificates are loaded
// (loadJunos) and before any tacplus TLS line: committed earlier, the image
// had written no group file under /var/db/aaa_certs/tacplus_tls and refused
// the tacplus tls trusted-ca-group line (attempts 9 and 11).
func l73TrustGroupLines() []string {
	return []string{
		"delete security pki trusted-ca-group " + l73TrustGroup,
		"set security pki trusted-ca-group " + l73TrustGroup + " ca-profiles " + l73RootProfile,
	}
}

// l73TrustDir is where the image looks for a TACACS+ tls trusted-ca-group
// (attempt 9's refusal names /var/db/aaa_certs/tacplus_tls/<group>). By
// analogy with Juniper's RADSEC page ("Configure RADIUS over TLS (RADSEC) for
// System Authentication"), which says a trusted-ca-group is a folder of CA
// certificates each named <openssl subject hash>.0; the TACACS+ page is silent.
const l73TrustDir = "/var/db/aaa_certs/tacplus_tls"

var l73HashName = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{8}$`)

// The two public CA certificates loadJunos uploads, and the only files the
// trust folder may be filled from.
const (
	l73RootOnDevice  = "/var/tmp/l73-root.pem"
	l73InterOnDevice = "/var/tmp/l73-inter.pem"
)

// l73TrustFolderCmds makes the group's folder on the device and copies each
// public CA certificate (source path on the device, openssl subject hash) into
// it as <hash>.0. Only l73RootOnDevice and l73InterOnDevice are accepted as a
// source (an allowlist: no key, no device certificate, no other path).
func l73TrustFolderCmds(group string, certs [][2]string) ([]string, error) {
	dir := l73TrustDir + "/" + group
	cmds := []string{"file make-directory " + dir}
	for _, c := range certs {
		src, hash := c[0], c[1]
		if !l73HashName.MatchString(hash) {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("subject hash %q is not eight lowercase hex digits", hash)
		}
		if src != l73RootOnDevice && src != l73InterOnDevice {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("%q is not one of the two uploaded CA certificates: nothing else goes into the trust folder", src)
		}
		cmds = append(cmds, "file copy "+src+" "+dir+"/"+hash+".0")
	}
	return cmds, nil
}

// l73SubjectHash is openssl's `x509 -noout -subject_hash` of a public
// certificate: the name the trust folder's file takes.
func l73SubjectHash(certPEM []byte) (string, error) {
	cmd := exec.Command("openssl", "x509", "-noout", "-subject_hash")
	cmd.Stdin = bytes.NewReader(certPEM)
	out, err := cmd.Output()
	if err != nil {
		return "", errors.New("openssl x509 -subject_hash failed")
	}
	h := strings.TrimSpace(string(out))
	if !l73HashName.MatchString(h) {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("openssl printed %q, not a subject hash", h)
	}
	return h, nil
}


// l73EnrolCommands are the documented offline enrolment's two operational
// commands: a key pair made on the device, and a request naming the device
// by its inventory name and address.
func l73EnrolCommands(node, addr string) (keyPair, csr string) {
	return "request security pki generate-key-pair certificate-id " + l73CertID,
		fmt.Sprintf(`request security pki generate-certificate-request certificate-id %s subject "CN=%s" domain-name %s ip-address %s filename %s`,
			l73CertID, node, node, addr, l73CSRPath)
}

func l73LoadCommands() []string {
	return []string{
		"request security pki ca-certificate load ca-profile " + l73RootProfile + " filename /var/tmp/l73-root.pem",
		"request security pki ca-certificate load ca-profile " + l73InterProfile + " filename /var/tmp/l73-inter.pem",
		"request security pki local-certificate load certificate-id " + l73CertID + " filename /var/tmp/l73-dev.crt",
	}
}

// l73Ask is one help request: typed, then "?" with no newline, then Ctrl-U.
// Control words prove the help is a real option list; Need are the
// keywords the harness will type at that level; Want are keywords it types
// only when the help lists them. Only, when set, asks this level only if an
// earlier ask listed that word.
//
// In and Out put the CLI in the mode the ask is meaningful in and back: In is
// typed before the ask and Out after its Ctrl-U, both only when set. They are
// for words typed outside the dialect's Enter mode (the AAA server group).
type l73Ask struct {
	Ask                 string
	Control, Need, Want []string
	Only                string
	In, Out             []string
}

// listed is the key under which a help's listing of word at this ask is
// recorded: by mode and level as well as word, so that two asks that look for
// the same word (IOS XE's AAA group and its `tls trustpoint` flag both look
// for `server`) never answer for each other. Only names such a key.
func (a l73Ask) listed(word string) string {
	return strings.Join(a.In, ";") + "@" + a.Ask + "|" + word
}

// l73RecordListed folds one ask's verdict (l73HelpVerdict's miss and have)
// into listed: a Need word is listed unless this help missed it, a Want word
// only if this help listed it.
func l73RecordListed(listed map[string]bool, a l73Ask, miss, have []string) {
	for _, w := range a.Need {
		k := a.listed(w)
		listed[k] = listed[k] || !slices.Contains(miss, w)
	}
	for _, w := range have {
		listed[a.listed(w)] = true
	}
}

// l73JunosAsks: the first is L71's own probe; the rest are asked only when
// it lists tls.
var l73JunosAsks = []l73Ask{
	{Ask: "set system tacplus-server 192.0.2.71 ", Control: []string{"secret", "port", "timeout"},
		Need: []string{"tls", "port", "single-connection", "routing-instance", "secret"}},
	{Ask: "set system tacplus-server 192.0.2.71 tls ", Control: []string{"trusted-ca-group"},
		Need: []string{"trusted-ca-group", "mutual-authentication"}},
	{Ask: "set system tacplus-server 192.0.2.71 tls mutual-authentication ", Control: []string{"certificate-id"},
		Need: []string{"certificate-id"}},
	{Ask: "set system accounting destination tacplus server 192.0.2.71 ", Control: []string{"secret", "port", "timeout"},
		Need: []string{"tls", "port", "routing-instance", "secret"}},
	{Ask: "set system accounting destination tacplus server 192.0.2.71 tls ", Control: []string{"trusted-ca-group"},
		Need: []string{"trusted-ca-group", "mutual-authentication"}},
	{Ask: "set system accounting destination tacplus server 192.0.2.71 tls mutual-authentication ", Control: []string{"certificate-id"},
		Need: []string{"certificate-id"}},
	{Ask: "set security pki ", Control: []string{"ca-profile"}, Need: []string{"ca-profile", "trusted-ca-group"}},
	{Ask: "set security pki ca-profile l73probe ", Control: []string{"ca-identity"}, Need: []string{"ca-identity", "revocation-check"}},
	{Ask: "set security pki ca-profile l73probe revocation-check ", Control: []string{"disable"}, Need: []string{"disable"}},
	{Ask: "set security pki trusted-ca-group l73probe ", Control: []string{"ca-profiles"}, Need: []string{"ca-profiles"}},
}

// l73HelpVerdict is the Need words help does not list, and the Want words it
// does. A help that lists none of the control words is an error: it is not
// an option list, and its lacking a word proves nothing.
func l73HelpVerdict(help string, a l73Ask) (miss, have []string, err error) {
	if _, ok := l71Says(help, a.Control); !ok {
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("INVALID: the help for `%s?` lists none of %v, so it is not an option list; its words: %s",
			strings.TrimSpace(a.Ask), a.Control, l71Words(help))
	}
	for _, w := range a.Need {
		if _, ok := l71Says(help, []string{w}); !ok {
			miss = append(miss, w)
		}
	}
	for _, w := range a.Want {
		if _, ok := l71Says(help, []string{w}); ok {
			have = append(have, w)
		}
	}
	return miss, have, nil
}

// l73Daemon is one cell's port49d: the lab config with the inventory
// replaced, an optional other RFC 8907 bind, the platform's session reply,
// a 2s backoff base, and a tls_listener.
type l73Daemon struct {
	Inventory     string
	Listen        string // "" keeps 172.100.100.1:49
	TLSListen     string
	Cert, Key     string
	ClientCA      string
	Intermediates string
	ServerNames   []string
	Tickets       time.Duration // session_ticket_lifetime; 0 is off (the default)
	StateDir      string
	Status        string // certificate_status, a YAML flow mapping
	FaultFile     string
	Attrs         string // session_attributes, a YAML flow list; "" keeps the lab file's
}

func l73DaemonYAML(base string, o l73Daemon) (string, error) {
	out, err := l73SetLine(base, "inventory_file: ", "inventory_file: "+o.Inventory)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	if o.Listen != "" {
		if out, err = l73SetLine(out, "listen: ", `listen: "`+o.Listen+`"`); err != nil {
			return "", err
		}
	}
	if o.Attrs != "" {
		if out, err = l73SetLine(out, "  session_attributes: ", "  session_attributes: "+o.Attrs); err != nil {
			return "", err
		}
	}
	if out, err = l73AddAfter(out, "limits:", "  malformed_backoff_base: 2s"); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	if o.FaultFile != "" {
		if out, err = l73AddAfter(out, "lab:", "  fault_file: "+o.FaultFile); err != nil {
			return "", err
		}
	}
	var b strings.Builder
	fmt.Fprintf(&b, "\ntls_listener:\n  listen: %q\n  cert_file: %s\n  key_file: %s\n  client_ca_file: %s\n",
		o.TLSListen, o.Cert, o.Key, o.ClientCA)
	if o.Intermediates != "" {
		fmt.Fprintf(&b, "  intermediates_file: %s\n", o.Intermediates)
	}
	if len(o.ServerNames) > 0 {
		quoted := make([]string, len(o.ServerNames))
		for i, n := range o.ServerNames {
			quoted[i] = strconv.Quote(n)
		}
		fmt.Fprintf(&b, "  server_names: [%s]\n", strings.Join(quoted, ", "))
	}
	fmt.Fprintf(&b, "  session_ticket_lifetime: %s\n  state_dir: %s\n  certificate_status: %s\n", o.Tickets, o.StateDir, o.Status)
	return out + b.String(), nil
}

// l73SetLine replaces the one line that begins with prefix.
func l73SetLine(cfg, prefix, line string) (string, error) {
	lines := strings.Split(cfg, "\n")
	n := 0
	for i, ln := range lines {
		if strings.HasPrefix(ln, prefix) {
			lines[i] = line
			n++
		}
	}
	if n != 1 {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("the lab config has %d lines beginning %q, want 1", n, prefix)
	}
	return strings.Join(lines, "\n"), nil
}

// l73AddAfter inserts line after the line that is exactly anchor.
func l73AddAfter(cfg, anchor, line string) (string, error) {
	lines := strings.Split(cfg, "\n")
	for i, ln := range lines {
		if ln == anchor {
			rest := append([]string{line}, lines[i+1:]...)
			return strings.Join(append(lines[:i+1], rest...), "\n"), nil
		}
	}
	return "", fmt.Errorf("the lab config has no %q line", anchor)
}

// l73Inventory marks device `transport: tls` in an inventory written with
// one flow mapping per device, as inventory-lab.yaml is.
func l73Inventory(base, device string) (string, error) {
	lines := strings.Split(base, "\n")
	found := false
	for i, ln := range lines {
		if !strings.Contains(ln, "{name: "+device+",") {
			continue
		}
		if strings.Contains(ln, "transport:") {
			return "", fmt.Errorf("the inventory's %s line already names a transport", device)
		}
		j := strings.LastIndex(ln, "}")
		if j < 0 {
			return "", fmt.Errorf("the inventory's %s line is not a flow mapping", device)
		}
		lines[i], found = ln[:j]+", transport: tls"+ln[j:], true
	}
	if !found {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("the inventory names no device %s", device)
	}
	return strings.Join(lines, "\n"), nil
}

// l73Cell is one L73 cell, as spec §15 words it.
type l73Cell struct{ ID, Claim string }

var l73Cells = []l73Cell{
	{"a", "the port it uses with none configured (RFC 9887 says 300; Cisco's documents use 6049)"},
	{"b", "whether every packet has the unencrypted flag set"},
	{"c", "whether it sends SNI, and what"},
	{"d", "whether it checks port49's certificate's dNSName or iPAddress against what it was configured with, and what it does on a mismatch and on an expired server certificate"},
	{"e", "the client certificate its documented enrolment issues: subjectAltName entries, extended key usage, chain sent"},
	{"f", "TLS 1.3 negotiated, and the suite"},
	{"g", "whether it resumes with a port49 ticket"},
	{"h", "single-connection over TLS"},
	{"i", "what it does on each port49 refusal (an alert at the handshake, a reset at Accept, a decline window, an ERROR for an obfuscated packet), and how fast it falls to its next server or local method, as L28 and L31 measured on 49"},
	{"j", "whether it ever falls back to a non-TLS server for the same host (RFC 9887 §5.1.1 forbids it)"},
}

// l73Obs is one cell's observation. Docs is "n/a", "yes", or "**no**: " and
// the document and sentence the observation contradicts.
type l73Obs struct{ ID, Configured, Observed, Docs string }

func l73RenderTable(obs []l73Obs) (string, error) {
	byID := map[string]l73Obs{}
	for _, o := range obs {
		if _, dup := byID[o.ID]; dup {
			return "", fmt.Errorf("cell %s observed twice", o.ID)
		}
		byID[o.ID] = o
	}
	esc := func(s string) string {
		return strings.ReplaceAll(strings.ReplaceAll(s, "|", `\|`), "\n", " ")
	}
	var b strings.Builder
	b.WriteString("| cell | claim | configured | observed | against the docs |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n")
	for _, c := range l73Cells {
		o, ok := byID[c.ID]
		if !ok {
			return "", fmt.Errorf("cell %s has no observation", c.ID)
		}
		delete(byID, c.ID)
		if strings.TrimSpace(o.Observed) == "" {
			return "", fmt.Errorf("cell %s has an empty observation: a blank row reads as measured", c.ID)
		}
		fmt.Fprintf(&b, "| %s | %s | %s | %s | %s |\n", c.ID, esc(c.Claim), esc(o.Configured), esc(o.Observed), esc(o.Docs))
	}
	for id := range byID {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("observation for unknown cell %q", id)
	}
	return b.String(), nil
}

// l73PEMBlock is the first PEM block on a CLI screen, line endings fixed and
// decoded once to prove it is one, of the wanted type ("CERTIFICATE REQUEST").
func l73PEMBlock(out, want string) ([]byte, error) {
	s := strings.ReplaceAll(out, "\r", "")
	i := strings.Index(s, "-----BEGIN ")
	if i < 0 {
		return nil, errors.New("no PEM block on the screen")
	}
	b, _ := pem.Decode([]byte(s[i:]))
	if b == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("a PEM header with no block after it")
	}
	if b.Type != want {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("the PEM block on the screen is a %s, want a %s", b.Type, want)
	}
	return pem.EncodeToMemory(b), nil
}

// scpSinkFrame is what an scp source sends a sink for one file: the C line,
// the bytes, the closing NUL.
func scpSinkFrame(name string, body []byte) []byte {
	var b bytes.Buffer
	fmt.Fprintf(&b, "C0644 %d %s\n", len(body), name)
	b.Write(body)
	b.WriteByte(0)
	return b.Bytes()
}

// l73MetricValue is the value of one exposition line, selector included, and
// whether the series exists: a misspelled selector is absent, not zero.
func l73MetricValue(exposition, selector string) (float64, bool) {
	for _, l := range strings.Split(exposition, "\n") {
		if strings.HasPrefix(l, selector+" ") {
			v, err := strconv.ParseFloat(strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(l, selector)), 64)
			if err == nil {
				return v, true
			}
		}
	}
	return 0, false
}

var (
	l73LogDrop = regexp.MustCompile(`(?i)password|token|secret|eyJ`)
	l73LogHash = regexp.MustCompile(`\$[0-9][a-z]?\$[^[:space:]"]*`)
	l73LogLong = regexp.MustCompile(`[A-Za-z0-9_+/=.-]{24,}`)
)

// l73ScrubLog is the lab's log-reading rule as code: drop every line naming
// a password, token, secret or JWT, mask every crypt or $9$ hash whatever its
// length, and shorten every long run.
func l73ScrubLog(s string) string {
	var out []string
	for _, ln := range strings.Split(s, "\n") {
		if l73LogDrop.MatchString(ln) {
			continue
		}
		if l73BarePath(ln) {
			out = append(out, ln)
			continue
		}
		if m := l73FilePathAnswer.FindStringSubmatchIndex(ln); m != nil && l73PrintablePath(ln[m[4]:m[5]]) {
			out = append(out, l73MaskLine(ln[:m[4]])+ln[m[4]:m[5]]+l73MaskLine(ln[m[5]:]))
			continue
		}
		out = append(out, l73MaskLine(ln))
	}
	return strings.Join(out, "\n")
}

func l73MaskLine(ln string) string {
	ln = l73LogHash.ReplaceAllString(ln, "<HASH>")
	return l73LogLong.ReplaceAllString(ln, "<LONG>")
}

var (
	l73FilePathAnswer = regexp.MustCompile(`(File path ')([^']*)(' doesn't exists?)`)
	l73FilePathShape  = regexp.MustCompile(`^/(var|config|etc|tmp|opt)/[A-Za-z0-9_./-]{1,200}$`)
)

// l73BarePath is true for a line that is only a printable path, as `file
// list` heads its listing ("<path>:") or the CLI echoes it ("file list
// <path> [recursive]").
func l73BarePath(ln string) bool {
	t := strings.TrimSpace(ln)
	if !strings.HasPrefix(t, "/") && !strings.HasPrefix(t, "file list /") {
		return false
	}
	t = strings.TrimPrefix(t, "file list ")
	t = strings.TrimSuffix(strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimSuffix(t, "recursive")), ":")
	return l73PrintablePath(t)
}

// l73PrintablePath is true for the one path shape Junos's "File path '<p>'
// doesn't exist" answer may show unmasked: under a directory the image keeps
// its files in, at most 200 characters, no segment over 64 (a base64 blob
// that happens to start with a slash is not one), and no "." or ".." segment.
func l73PrintablePath(p string) bool {
	if !l73FilePathShape.MatchString(p) {
		return false
	}
	for _, seg := range strings.Split(p, "/") {
		if len(seg) > 64 || seg == "." || seg == ".." {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

// l73ControlInvalid is the INVALID message for a control login that did not
// pass: the cause, the hint, the daemon's records and the TLS summary the
// cell would have printed. summary is the caller's obs with the lab keys
// already hidden (hideKeys lives in a lab-tagged file). It goes through the
// note's leak gate; if it names a leak, only the leak's name is printed. The
// gate covers the summary alone: errText is l73Err (scrubbed) and records is
// e.quote (credential hash redacted), as in the PASS note.
func l73ControlInvalid(errText, hint, records, summary string) string {
	text := "summary: " + summary
	if leaks := l73SummaryLeaks(summary); len(leaks) > 0 {
		text = "summary withheld: " + strings.Join(leaks, ", ")
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf("INVALID: the control login over TLS did not pass (%s)%s; records: %s; %s", errText, hint, records, text)
}

// l73PEMDropRe matches a PEM block whole, its label any RFC 7468 text
// (letters, digits, spaces, dots, hyphens), or to the end of the text when
// the block is unterminated.
var l73PEMDropRe = regexp.MustCompile(`(?s)-----BEGIN [A-Za-z0-9 .-]+-----.*?(?:-----END [A-Za-z0-9 .-]+-----|\z)`)

// l73DropPEM removes every PEM block, certificate or key, whole: a public
// certificate's fields are readable above the block, and a key must never
// reach a file.
func l73DropPEM(s string) string {
	return l73PEMDropRe.ReplaceAllString(s, "<PEM block dropped>")
}

var l73Space = regexp.MustCompile(`\s+`)

// l73NormSet is a `set` line as `display set` would spell it: quotes gone,
// whitespace single.
func l73NormSet(ln string) string {
	return l73Space.ReplaceAllString(strings.TrimSpace(strings.ReplaceAll(ln, `"`, "")), " ")
}

// l73Hierarchy is the configuration path a `set` line lives under, as
// `show configuration <path> | display set` takes it: its first two words.
func l73Hierarchy(ln string) string {
	f := strings.Fields(ln)
	if len(f) < 3 || f[0] != "set" {
		return ""
	}
	return f[1] + " " + f[2]
}

// l73MissingLines names every typed `set` line that shown (the `display set`
// output of the hierarchies they live under) does not hold. A secret line is
// compared by presence alone, since the device shows it encrypted: it is
// found by the words before the secret, and named without its value.
//
// It checks presence only. It does not consult a `deactivate` line in shown
// (an inactive committed statement counts as present), and it cannot tell that
// a `delete` failed silently and left a stale statement. Neither applies to
// the lines typed today; extend it before the line set grows to need either.
func l73MissingLines(typed []string, shown string) []string {
	have := map[string]bool{}
	var secretPrefixes []string
	for _, ln := range strings.Split(shown, "\n") {
		n := l73NormSet(ln)
		have[n] = true
		if i := strings.Index(n, " secret "); i >= 0 {
			secretPrefixes = append(secretPrefixes, n[:i])
		}
	}
	var missing []string
	for _, ln := range typed {
		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(ln), "set ") {
			continue
		}
		n := l73NormSet(ln)
		if i := strings.Index(n, " secret "); i >= 0 {
			if !slices.Contains(secretPrefixes, n[:i]) {
				missing = append(missing, n[:i]+" secret <masked>")
			}
			continue
		}
		if !have[n] {
			missing = append(missing, n)
		}
	}
	return missing
}

// l73RefusedDir is the directory of the path in a "File path '<p>' doesn't
// exist" answer, when the path may be printed (l73PrintablePath) and lies
// under /var or /config; otherwise "".
func l73RefusedDir(out string) string {
	m := l73FilePathAnswer.FindStringSubmatch(out)
	if m == nil || !l73PrintablePath(m[2]) {
		return ""
	}
	if !strings.HasPrefix(m[2], "/var/") && !strings.HasPrefix(m[2], "/config/") {
		return ""
	}
	return m[2][:strings.LastIndex(m[2], "/")]
}
```

### Task 6a: Dialects: the IOS XE, NX-OS and EOS lines, help asks and enrolment

Everything platform-specific that `TestL73` types or reads, as data. The Junos entries wrap Task 6's lines. The IOS XE and NX-OS lines follow Cisco docs 225097 and 224882, translated onto the lab's startup configs: group `port49`, VRF `clab-mgmt` and `GigabitEthernet1` on IOS XE, `use-vrf management` on NX-OS. The EOS lines follow Arista's manual: an SSL profile under `management security`, named by the global `tacacs-server tls ssl-profile <name>`, with a bare `tls` on the host line (cEOS 4.36.3M's own help, Task 14 attempts 1 to 3; the code blocks below still show the first guess, `host … ssl-profile`, and `l73_dialect.go` is the truth). The EOS asks confirm `tls`, the global `ssl-profile`, and the profile submode's `certificate <crt> key`, `chain certificate` and `trust certificate` before anything is typed, entering a scratch profile `l73x` and deleting it. After each configure the harness reads `show running-config section tacacs` back and is INVALID naming a typed line it does not show (`l73EOSMissing`; key words are never compared or named). A profile keyword the image spells differently is refused at its first configure, which makes the run INVALID with the screen kept. The enrolment prompts are the ones the documents show. A prompt this table does not know makes the run INVALID with the scrubbed screen (Task 7a), and the fix is a new step here.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/lab/l73_dialect.go`, `tests/lab/l73_dialect_unit_test.go`
- Modify: `tests/lab/l71_probes.go` (three entries), `tests/lab/l71_unit_test.go` (`TestL71ProbesEveryPlatform`'s list)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6's constants, `l73DeviceTLS`, `l73Ask`, `l73JunosAsks`; `l71Probes`; `labcreds.Key`.
- Produces:
  - `type l73Dialect struct{…}` and `l73Dialects map[string]l73Dialect`, keyed by platform name.
  - `l73CiscoLines(family string, prev *l73DeviceTLS, d l73DeviceTLS, has map[string]bool) []string`, `l73CiscoUndo(family string, d l73DeviceTLS, has map[string]bool) []string` and `l73CiscoRestoreAll(family string, has map[string]bool) []string`.
  - `type l73Step struct{ Prompt, Reply string }`, `type l73Dialog struct{ Cmd string; Steps []l73Step; Done string }`, `l73Lines(lines ...string) []l73Dialog` and `l73CiscoEnrol(family, node string) (prep []l73Dialog, enroll, load l73Dialog)`.
  - `l73EOSEnrol(node string) (keyGen, csr string, load, profile []string)` and the constant `l73EOSProfile`.
  - `l73NextAnswer(tail string, steps []l73Step, used []bool, prompt string) (step int, ok bool)`, `l73Expand(reply string, vals map[string]string) string` and `l73Refusal(out string) string`.
  - `l71Probes["arista_ceos"]`, `l71Probes["cisco_iol-xe"]` and `l71Probes["cisco_xrd"]`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** `tests/lab/l73_dialect_unit_test.go`:
```go
package lab

import (
	"os"
	"regexp"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/blanketfish/port49/tests/lab/labcreds"
)

// Each dialect's first ask is L71's own question, entered and left the way
// L71 enters and leaves it, so the addendum answers L71's question and not
// a neighbour of it.
func TestL73DialectsAskL71sQuestionFirst(t *testing.T) {
	for name, d := range l73Dialects {
		p, ok := l71Probes[d.Probe]
		if !ok {
			t.Errorf("%s: probe %q is not in l71Probes", name, d.Probe)
			continue
		}
		if len(d.Asks) == 0 || !slices.Contains(p.ask, d.Asks[0].Ask) {
			t.Errorf("%s: first ask %+v is not L71's question", name, d.Asks)
		}
		// L71's client words are each looked for by some ask, so the verdict
		// that the image has a client and the keywords typed agree.
		for _, y := range p.yes {
			if !slices.ContainsFunc(d.Asks, func(a l73Ask) bool { return slices.Contains(a.Need, y) || slices.Contains(a.Want, y) }) {
				t.Errorf("%s: L71's client word %q is asked by no ask", name, y)
			}
		}
		if !slices.Equal(d.Enter, p.enter) || !slices.Equal(d.Cleanup, p.cleanup) || d.CfgMark == "" {
			t.Errorf("%s: enter %q cleanup %q, L71's %q %q", name, d.Enter, d.Cleanup, p.enter, p.cleanup)
		}
		if !d.HelpOnly && (d.VersionCmd == "" || d.VersionHas == "" || d.LogCmd == "" || len(d.ShowCmds) == 0 ||
			d.Enrol == "" || d.Fallback == "" || d.Revocation == "") {
			t.Errorf("%s: a measured dialect with an empty field: %+v", name, d)
		}
		for _, a := range d.Asks {
			if a.Only != "" && !slices.ContainsFunc(d.Asks, func(b l73Ask) bool {
				return slices.Contains(b.Need, a.Only) || slices.Contains(b.Want, a.Only)
			}) {
				t.Errorf("%s: %q is asked only after %q, which no ask looks for", name, a.Ask, a.Only)
			}
		}
	}
}

func TestL73IOSXELines(t *testing.T) {
	got := l73CiscoLines("iosxe", nil, l73DeviceTLS{Server: "172.100.100.1"}, nil)
	want := []string{
		"tacacs server l73-tls", "address ipv4 172.100.100.1", "tls trustpoint client l73-dev",
		"tls ip vrf forwarding clab-mgmt", "tls ip tacacs source-interface GigabitEthernet1", "exit",
		"aaa group server tacacs+ port49", "no server name port49-host", "server name l73-tls", "exit",
	}
	if !slices.Equal(got, want) {
		t.Fatalf("got\n%s", strings.Join(got, "\n"))
	}
	prev := l73DeviceTLS{Server: "172.100.100.1"}
	got = l73CiscoLines("iosxe", &prev, l73DeviceTLS{Server: "172.100.100.1", Port: 300, SingleConnection: true,
		Second: "172.100.100.2"}, map[string]bool{l73HasTrustpointServer: true})
	for _, line := range []string{"no tacacs server l73-tls", "tls port 300", "single-connection",
		"tls trustpoint server l73-root", "tacacs server l73-second", "key 0 labsecret", "server name l73-second"} {
		if !slices.Contains(got, line) {
			t.Errorf("missing %q", line)
		}
	}
	// The undo comes first, whole, before anything of the new cell.
	if i, j := slices.Index(got, "no tacacs server l73-tls"), slices.Index(got, "tls port 300"); i < 0 || j < i {
		t.Errorf("undo at %d, new lines at %d", i, j)
	}
}

func TestL73NXOSLines(t *testing.T) {
	got := l73CiscoLines("nxos", nil, l73DeviceTLS{Server: "172.100.100.1", Port: 300}, map[string]bool{l73HasNXOSSecure: true})
	want := []string{
		"aaa group server tacacs+ port49", "no server 172.100.100.1", "exit", "no tacacs-server host 172.100.100.1",
		"tacacs-server secure tls",
		"tacacs-server host 172.100.100.1 port 300", "tacacs-server host 172.100.100.1 tls client-trustpoint l73-dev",
		"aaa group server tacacs+ port49", "server 172.100.100.1", "exit",
	}
	if !slices.Equal(got, want) {
		t.Fatalf("got\n%s", strings.Join(got, "\n"))
	}
	got = l73CiscoLines("nxos", nil, l73DeviceTLS{Server: "172.100.100.1", Second: "172.100.100.2"}, nil)
	if slices.Contains(got, "tacacs-server secure tls") || !slices.Contains(got, "tacacs-server host 172.100.100.2 key 0 labsecret") ||
		slices.Contains(got, "tacacs-server host 172.100.100.2 tls client-trustpoint l73-dev") {
		t.Fatalf("%q", got)
	}
}

func TestL73EOSLines(t *testing.T) {
	got := l73CiscoLines("eos", nil, l73DeviceTLS{Server: "172.100.100.1"}, nil)
	want := []string{
		"aaa group server tacacs+ port49", "no server 172.100.100.1", "exit", "no tacacs-server host 172.100.100.1",
		"tacacs-server host 172.100.100.1 ssl-profile l73-tls",
		"aaa group server tacacs+ port49", "server 172.100.100.1", "exit",
	}
	if !slices.Equal(got, want) {
		t.Fatalf("got\n%s", strings.Join(got, "\n"))
	}
	got = l73CiscoLines("eos", nil, l73DeviceTLS{Server: "172.100.100.1", Port: 300, SingleConnection: true,
		Second: "172.100.100.2"}, map[string]bool{l73HasNXOSSecure: true})
	for _, line := range []string{"tacacs-server host 172.100.100.1 single-connection port 300 ssl-profile l73-tls",
		"tacacs-server host 172.100.100.2 key 0 labsecret", "server 172.100.100.2"} {
		if !slices.Contains(got, line) {
			t.Errorf("missing %q", line)
		}
	}
	if slices.Contains(got, "tacacs-server secure tls") || slices.Contains(l73CiscoRestoreAll("eos", map[string]bool{l73HasNXOSSecure: true}), "no tacacs-server secure tls") {
		t.Fatal("an NX-OS keyword typed on EOS")
	}
	_, csr, load, profile := l73EOSEnrol("leaf1")
	if !strings.Contains(csr, "common-name leaf1") || len(load) != 3 || profile[0] != "management security" ||
		!slices.Contains(profile, "ssl profile l73-tls") || !slices.Contains(profile, "chain certificate l73-inter.crt") {
		t.Fatalf("%q %q %q", csr, load, profile)
	}
}

// The undo ends in the startup config's own lines, read from the files, so
// a renamed server or group in a startup config fails here and not on the
// device.
func TestL73CiscoUndoIsTheStartupConfig(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tc := range []struct{ family, file string }{
		{"iosxe", "configs/iosxe-startup.cfg"}, {"nxos", "configs/nxos-startup.cfg"}, {"eos", "configs/eos-startup.cfg"},
	} {
		b, err := os.ReadFile(tc.file)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		startup := map[string]bool{}
		for _, ln := range strings.Split(string(b), "\n") {
			startup[strings.TrimSpace(ln)] = true
		}
		has := map[string]bool{l73HasNXOSSecure: true}
		undo := l73CiscoRestoreAll(tc.family, has)
		for _, ln := range undo {
			if strings.HasPrefix(ln, "no ") || ln == "exit" {
				continue
			}
			if !startup[ln] {
				t.Errorf("%s: the undo types %q, which %s does not hold", tc.family, ln, tc.file)
			}
		}
		apply := l73CiscoLines(tc.family, nil, l73DeviceTLS{Server: l73Server, Second: l73SecondServer, SecondTLS: true}, has)
		for _, ln := range apply {
			if strings.HasPrefix(ln, "tacacs server ") && !slices.Contains(undo, "no "+ln) {
				t.Errorf("%s: %q is never removed", tc.family, ln)
			}
			if strings.HasPrefix(ln, "tacacs-server host ") && !slices.Contains(undo, "no "+strings.Join(strings.Fields(ln)[:3], " ")) {
				t.Errorf("%s: %q is never removed", tc.family, ln)
			}
			if ln == "tacacs-server secure tls" && !slices.Contains(undo, "no tacacs-server secure tls") {
				t.Errorf("%s: secure tls is never removed", tc.family)
			}
		}
	}
}

func TestL73NextAnswer(t *testing.T) {
	steps := []l73Step{{"serial number", "no"}, {"IP address", "no"}}
	used := []bool{false, false}
	if _, ok := l73NextAnswer("% Start certificate enrollment ..\r\n", steps, used, "xe1("); ok {
		t.Fatal("an answer before any question")
	}
	tail := "% Include an IP address in the subject name? [no]: and the serial number in the subject name? "
	if n, ok := l73NextAnswer(tail, steps, used, "xe1("); !ok || n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("%d %v: the earliest question is the IP address one", n, ok)
	}
	used[1] = true
	if n, ok := l73NextAnswer(tail, steps, used, "xe1("); !ok || n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("%d %v: an answered question chosen again", n, ok)
	}
	if n, ok := l73NextAnswer("done\r\nxe1(config)#", steps, used, "xe1("); !ok || n != -1 {
		t.Fatalf("%d %v: the prompt not read as the end", n, ok)
	}
	if n, ok := l73NextAnswer("xe1(config)# serial number", steps, []bool{false, false}, "xe1("); !ok || n != -1 {
		t.Fatalf("%d %v: a question after the prompt chosen over it", n, ok)
	}
}

func TestL73Expand(t *testing.T) {
	got := l73Expand("{ROOT}{INTER}quit", map[string]string{"ROOT": "R\n", "INTER": "I\n"})
	if got != "R\nI\nquit" {
		t.Fatalf("%q", got)
	}
}

// No reply names a placeholder l73Expand does not fill, and no reply is a
// secret: the dialogues type public certificates, names and yes or no.
func TestL73DialogsUseKnownPlaceholders(t *testing.T) {
	ph := regexp.MustCompile(`\{[A-Z]+\}`)
	for _, family := range []string{"iosxe", "nxos"} {
		prep, enroll, load := l73CiscoEnrol(family, "xe1")
		for _, d := range append(append(prep, enroll), load) {
			for _, s := range d.Steps {
				for _, m := range ph.FindAllString(s.Reply, -1) {
					if !slices.Contains([]string{"{ROOT}", "{INTER}", "{LEAF}", "{NODE}", "{ADDR}"}, m) {
						t.Errorf("%s %q: unknown placeholder %s", family, d.Cmd, m)
					}
				}
				if strings.Contains(s.Reply, labcreds.Key) || strings.Contains(s.Reply, "breakglass") {
					t.Errorf("%s %q: a reply carries a secret", family, d.Cmd)
				}
			}
		}
		if enroll.Done == "" {
			t.Errorf("%s: the enrolment has no Done marker", family)
		}
	}
}

func TestL73Refusal(t *testing.T) {
	if got := l73Refusal("tls port 300\r\n          ^\r\n% Invalid input detected at '^' marker.\r\n"); got != "% Invalid input detected at '^' marker." {
		t.Fatalf("%q", got)
	}
	if got := l73Refusal("xe1(config-server-tacacs)#"); got != "" {
		t.Fatalf("%q", got)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement** `tests/lab/l73_dialect.go`:
```go
package lab

import (
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	"github.com/blanketfish/port49/tests/lab/labcreds"
)

// l73Dialect is how TestL73 asks one platform's CLI about its RFC 9887
// client, points the client at port49 and puts it back, and reads it.
type l73Dialect struct {
	Family      string // junos, iosxe, nxos, eos, xr: which line builders apply
	Vendor      string // the note's name for the OS
	Probe       string // the l71Probes entry whose question the first ask is
	Enter       []string
	Cleanup     []string
	CfgMark     string // what every configuration prompt contains
	Asks        []l73Ask
	HelpOnly    bool
	VersionCmd  string // run after each token login
	VersionHas  string // what its output holds on the image under test
	Attrs       string // port49d's session_attributes here; "" keeps the lab file's
	Enrol       string // cell e's "configured" wording
	Fallback    string // the startup config's login method list (cell i)
	Revocation  string // the run facts' sentence on device-side revocation
	LogCmd      string
	ShowCmds    []string
	InvalidCmds []string // read into PORT49_L73_INVALID_DIR when the control login fails
	RefusalCmds []string // read into the INVALID dir when configuring the TLS client is refused
	DiagOpAsks  []string // DiagAsks' operational-mode counterpart
	DiagAsks    []string // help asked at a value position by PORT49_L73_DIAG=help, which then ends INVALID
}

var l73JunosDialect = l73Dialect{
	Family: "junos", Vendor: "Junos", Probe: "juniper_vjunos-router",
	Enter: []string{"configure"}, Cleanup: []string{"rollback 0", "exit configuration-mode"}, CfgMark: "# ",
	Asks: l73JunosAsks, VersionCmd: "show version | match Junos:", VersionHas: "26.2R1", Attrs: "[]",
	Enrol: "the documented offline enrolment (`request security pki generate-key-pair`, " +
		"`generate-certificate-request`, the lab CA's answer loaded back from a file)",
	Fallback:    "`authentication-order [ tacplus password ]` (configs/vjunos-startup.conf)",
	Revocation:  "`revocation-check disable` on its CA profiles",
	LogCmd:      `show log messages | match "tacplus|TACPLUS|tls|TLS|certif|pki" | last 40`,
	RefusalCmds: []string{"show security pki ca-certificate", "show configuration security pki | display set"},
	DiagOpAsks:  []string{"request security pki ", "request system ", "show security pki ", "request security pki ca-certificate "},
	DiagAsks:    []string{"set security pki trusted-ca-group " + l73TrustGroup + " "},
	InvalidCmds: []string{`show log messages | match "tacplus|tls|ssl|pki" | last 60`,
		"show security pki local-certificate detail",
		"show configuration system tacplus-server | display set",
		"show configuration system accounting | display set"},
	ShowCmds: []string{"show configuration system tacplus-server | display set",
		"show configuration system accounting | display set", "show configuration security pki | display set",
		"show security pki local-certificate certificate-id " + l73CertID + " detail"},
}

// IOS XE's help is asked inside a probe server object, as L71 asked it.
var l73IOSXEAsks = []l73Ask{
	{Ask: "", Control: []string{"address", "key", "port", "timeout"}, Need: []string{"tls", "address", "single-connection", "key", "port"}},
	{Ask: "address ", Control: []string{"ipv4"}, Need: []string{"ipv4"}},
	{Ask: "tls ", Control: []string{"port", "trustpoint", "idle-timeout", "connection-timeout"}, Need: []string{"port", "trustpoint", "ip"}},
	{Ask: "tls trustpoint ", Control: []string{"client", "server"}, Need: []string{"client"}, Want: []string{"server"}},
	{Ask: "tls ip ", Control: []string{"vrf", "tacacs"}, Need: []string{"vrf", "tacacs"}},
	{Ask: "tls ip vrf ", Control: []string{"forwarding"}, Need: []string{"forwarding"}},
	{Ask: "tls ip tacacs ", Control: []string{"source-interface"}, Need: []string{"source-interface"}},
	// The AAA server group the cell's servers join: IOS XE leaves the probe
	// object, enters the startup config's group and comes back.
	{Ask: "", Control: []string{"server"}, Need: []string{"server"},
		In: []string{"exit", "aaa group server tacacs+ " + l73Group}, Out: []string{"exit", "tacacs server l71probe"}},
	{Ask: "server ", Control: []string{"name"}, Need: []string{"name"},
		In: []string{"exit", "aaa group server tacacs+ " + l73Group}, Out: []string{"exit", "tacacs server l71probe"}},
}

var l73IOSXEDialect = l73Dialect{
	Family: "iosxe", Vendor: "IOS XE", Probe: "cisco_iosxe",
	Enter: []string{"configure terminal", "tacacs server l71probe"}, Cleanup: []string{"exit", "no tacacs server l71probe", "end"},
	CfgMark: "(config", Asks: l73IOSXEAsks,
	VersionCmd: "show version | include Cisco IOS XE Software", VersionHas: "17.18", Attrs: `["priv-lvl=15"]`,
	Enrol: "terminal enrolment as Cisco doc 225097 shows it (`crypto key generate ec keysize 256`, `crypto pki enroll`, " +
		"the request read off the screen, the lab CA's answer pasted at `crypto pki import`)",
	Fallback:   "`aaa authentication login default group port49 local` (configs/iosxe-startup.cfg)",
	Revocation: "`revocation-check none` on both trustpoints",
	LogCmd:     "show logging | include TACACS|TLS|PKI|SSL|CRYPTO",
	ShowCmds: []string{"show running-config | section tacacs", "show running-config | section crypto pki trustpoint",
		"show crypto pki certificates " + l73CertID, "show tacacs"},
}

var l73NXOSAsks = []l73Ask{
	{Ask: "tacacs-server host 192.0.2.71 ", Control: []string{"key", "port", "timeout"}, Need: []string{"tls", "port", "single-connection", "key"}},
	{Ask: "tacacs-server ", Control: []string{"host", "key", "timeout"}, Need: []string{"host"}, Want: []string{"secure"}},
	{Ask: "tacacs-server secure ", Control: []string{"tls"}, Need: []string{"tls"}, Only: l73HasNXOSSecure},
	{Ask: "tacacs-server host 192.0.2.71 tls ", Control: []string{"client-trustpoint", "server-trustpoint"},
		Need: []string{"client-trustpoint"}, Want: []string{"server-trustpoint"}},
	{Ask: "", Control: []string{"server"}, Need: []string{"server"}, In: []string{"aaa group server tacacs+ " + l73Group}, Out: []string{"exit"}},
}

// EOS: the client keyword is ssl-profile (L71 looked for it and for tls);
// the profile itself is a submode the help cannot be asked about.
var l73EOSDialect = l73Dialect{
	Family: "eos", Vendor: "EOS", Probe: "arista_ceos",
	Enter: []string{"configure"}, Cleanup: []string{"end"}, CfgMark: "(config",
	Asks: []l73Ask{{Ask: "tacacs-server host 192.0.2.71 ", Control: []string{"key", "port", "timeout"},
		Need: []string{"ssl-profile", "port", "single-connection", "key"}, Want: []string{"tls"}},
		{Ask: "", Control: []string{"server"}, Need: []string{"server"}, In: []string{"aaa group server tacacs+ " + l73Group}, Out: []string{"exit"}}},
	VersionCmd: "show version | include Software image version", VersionHas: "4.36.3M",
	Enrol: "EOS's documented enrolment (`security pki key generate`, `security pki certificate generate signing-request`, " +
		"the request read off the screen, the lab CA's answer copied to `certificate:`; the SSL profile names the " +
		"intermediate as its `chain certificate`)",
	Fallback:   "`aaa authentication login default group port49 local` (configs/eos-startup.cfg)",
	Revocation: "no CRL in its SSL profile",
	LogCmd:     "show logging | include TACACS|TLS|SSL|AAA",
	ShowCmds: []string{"show running-config section tacacs", "show running-config section management security",
		"show management security ssl profile " + l73EOSProfile, "show tacacs"},
}

var l73Dialects = map[string]l73Dialect{
	"juniper_vjunos-router": l73JunosDialect,
	"arista_ceos":           l73EOSDialect,
	"juniper_vjunos-switch": l73JunosDialect,
	"cisco_iosxe":           l73IOSXEDialect,
	"cisco_nxos": {
		Family: "nxos", Vendor: "NX-OS", Probe: "cisco_nxos",
		Enter: []string{"configure terminal"}, Cleanup: []string{"end"}, CfgMark: "(config", Asks: l73NXOSAsks,
		VersionCmd: "show version | include NXOS:", VersionHas: "10.6(2)",
		Enrol: "terminal enrolment as Cisco doc 224882 shows it (`crypto key generate ecc`, `crypto ca enroll`, " +
			"the request read off the screen, the lab CA's answer pasted at `crypto ca import`)",
		Fallback:   "`aaa authentication login default group port49 local` (configs/nxos-startup.cfg)",
		Revocation: "`revocation-check none` on both trustpoints",
		LogCmd:     `show logging logfile | egrep "TACACS|tacacs|TLS|tls|PKI|pki" | last 40`,
		ShowCmds: []string{"show running-config tacacs+", "show crypto ca trustpoints",
			"show crypto ca certificates " + l73CertID, "show tacacs-server"},
	},
	"cisco_iol-xe": {Family: "iosxe", Vendor: "IOS XE (IOL)", Probe: "cisco_iol-xe", HelpOnly: true,
		Enter: l73IOSXEDialect.Enter, Cleanup: l73IOSXEDialect.Cleanup, CfgMark: "(config", Asks: l73IOSXEAsks},
	"cisco_xrd": {Family: "xr", Vendor: "IOS XR (XRd)", Probe: "cisco_xrd", HelpOnly: true,
		Enter: []string{"configure"}, Cleanup: []string{"abort"}, CfgMark: "(config",
		Asks: []l73Ask{{Ask: "tacacs-server host 192.0.2.71 ", Control: []string{"key", "port", "timeout"}, Need: []string{"tls"}}}},
}

// The names the Cisco lines use. l73XEStart and l73Group are the startup
// configs' (TestL73CiscoUndoIsTheStartupConfig reads them from the files).
const (
	l73XEServer = "l73-tls"
	l73XESecond = "l73-second"
	l73XEStart  = "port49-host"
	l73Group    = "port49"
	l73XEVRF    = "clab-mgmt"
	l73XESource = "GigabitEthernet1"
	l73CiscoKey = "l73-key"
)

// l73RecoverLines is what to type when an ask's In or Out failed part way and
// the CLI's mode is unknown: leave configuration with the dialect's own last
// cleanup line (the first line is typed expecting the exec prompt), then
// enter as the probe did, so the cleanup runs from the mode it assumes. Nil
// when no ask leaves the Enter mode.
func l73RecoverLines(d l73Dialect) []string {
	if len(d.Cleanup) == 0 || !slices.ContainsFunc(d.Asks, func(a l73Ask) bool { return len(a.In) > 0 || len(a.Out) > 0 }) {
		return nil
	}
	return append([]string{d.Cleanup[len(d.Cleanup)-1]}, d.Enter...)
}

// The keys of the Want words the Cisco lines read from has (l73RecordListed).
var (
	l73HasTrustpointServer = l73Ask{Ask: "tls trustpoint "}.listed("server")
	l73HasNXOSSecure       = l73Ask{Ask: "tacacs-server "}.listed("secure")
	l73HasNXOSServerTrust  = l73Ask{Ask: "tacacs-server host 192.0.2.71 tls "}.listed("server-trustpoint")
)

// l73CiscoLines is what one Cisco cell types: the previous cell's undo (prev
// nil: the startup config is in place), then this cell's lines. IOS XE and
// NX-OS have no commit to roll back, so each cell starts from the startup
// config's servers. has is the Want words the image's help listed.
func l73CiscoLines(family string, prev *l73DeviceTLS, d l73DeviceTLS, has map[string]bool) []string {
	var out []string
	if prev != nil {
		out = l73CiscoUndo(family, *prev, has)
	}
	switch family {
	case "iosxe":
		return append(out, l73IOSXEApply(d, has)...)
	case "nxos":
		return append(out, l73NXOSApply(d, has)...)
	case "eos":
		return append(out, l73EOSApply(d)...)
	}
	return nil
}

// l73CiscoUndo takes d's servers away and puts the startup config's back.
func l73CiscoUndo(family string, d l73DeviceTLS, has map[string]bool) []string {
	switch family {
	case "iosxe":
		out := []string{"aaa group server tacacs+ " + l73Group, "no server name " + l73XEServer}
		if d.Second != "" {
			out = append(out, "no server name "+l73XESecond)
		}
		out = append(out, "server name "+l73XEStart, "exit", "no tacacs server "+l73XEServer)
		if d.Second != "" {
			out = append(out, "no tacacs server "+l73XESecond)
		}
		return out
	case "nxos", "eos":
		out := []string{"aaa group server tacacs+ " + l73Group, "no server " + d.Server}
		if d.Second != "" {
			out = append(out, "no server "+d.Second)
		}
		out = append(out, "exit", "no tacacs-server host "+d.Server)
		if d.Second != "" {
			out = append(out, "no tacacs-server host "+d.Second)
		}
		if family == "nxos" && has[l73HasNXOSSecure] {
			out = append(out, "no tacacs-server secure tls")
		}
		return append(out, "tacacs-server host "+l73Server+" key 0 "+labcreds.Key,
			"aaa group server tacacs+ "+l73Group, "server "+l73Server, "exit")
	}
	return nil
}

// l73CiscoRestoreAll is the cleanup's undo: every server L73 can configure.
// It is typed leniently (Task 7a), because the cell that failed may have
// applied only some of its lines.
func l73CiscoRestoreAll(family string, has map[string]bool) []string {
	return l73CiscoUndo(family, l73DeviceTLS{Server: l73Server, Second: l73SecondServer}, has)
}

func l73IOSXEServer(name, addr string, tls bool, port int, single, secret, serverTrust bool) []string {
	out := []string{"tacacs server " + name, "address ipv4 " + addr}
	switch {
	case tls:
		if port != 0 {
			out = append(out, "tls port "+strconv.Itoa(port))
		}
		out = append(out, "tls trustpoint client "+l73CertID)
		if serverTrust {
			out = append(out, "tls trustpoint server "+l73RootProfile)
		}
		out = append(out, "tls ip vrf forwarding "+l73XEVRF, "tls ip tacacs source-interface "+l73XESource)
	case port != 0:
		out = append(out, "port "+strconv.Itoa(port))
	}
	if single {
		out = append(out, "single-connection")
	}
	if secret {
		out = append(out, "key 0 "+labcreds.Key)
	}
	return append(out, "exit")
}

func l73IOSXEApply(d l73DeviceTLS, has map[string]bool) []string {
	out := l73IOSXEServer(l73XEServer, d.Server, true, d.Port, d.SingleConnection, d.Secret, has[l73HasTrustpointServer])
	if d.Second != "" {
		out = append(out, l73IOSXEServer(l73XESecond, d.Second, d.SecondTLS, d.SecondPort, false,
			d.Secret || !d.SecondTLS, has[l73HasTrustpointServer])...)
	}
	out = append(out, "aaa group server tacacs+ "+l73Group, "no server name "+l73XEStart, "server name "+l73XEServer)
	if d.Second != "" {
		out = append(out, "server name "+l73XESecond)
	}
	return append(out, "exit")
}

func l73NXOSHost(addr string, tls bool, port int, single, secret bool, has map[string]bool) []string {
	h := "tacacs-server host " + addr
	first := h
	if secret {
		first += " key 0 " + labcreds.Key
	}
	if port != 0 {
		first += " port " + strconv.Itoa(port)
	}
	if single {
		first += " single-connection"
	}
	out := []string{first}
	if tls {
		out = append(out, h+" tls client-trustpoint "+l73CertID)
		if has[l73HasNXOSServerTrust] {
			out = append(out, h+" tls server-trustpoint "+l73RootProfile)
		}
	}
	return out
}

// l73NXOSApply starts from the startup config: the plain host leaves the
// group and is removed before the TLS host takes its address.
func l73NXOSApply(d l73DeviceTLS, has map[string]bool) []string {
	out := []string{"aaa group server tacacs+ " + l73Group, "no server " + l73Server, "exit", "no tacacs-server host " + l73Server}
	if has[l73HasNXOSSecure] {
		out = append(out, "tacacs-server secure tls")
	}
	out = append(out, l73NXOSHost(d.Server, true, d.Port, d.SingleConnection, d.Secret, has)...)
	if d.Second != "" {
		out = append(out, l73NXOSHost(d.Second, d.SecondTLS, d.SecondPort, false, d.Secret || !d.SecondTLS, has)...)
	}
	out = append(out, "aaa group server tacacs+ "+l73Group, "server "+d.Server)
	if d.Second != "" {
		out = append(out, "server "+d.Second)
	}
	return append(out, "exit")
}

// l73EOSProfile is the SSL profile the EOS host line names.
const l73EOSProfile = "l73-tls"

func l73EOSHost(addr string, tls bool, port int, single, secret bool) string {
	line := "tacacs-server host " + addr
	if single {
		line += " single-connection"
	}
	if port != 0 {
		line += " port " + strconv.Itoa(port)
	}
	if tls {
		line += " ssl-profile " + l73EOSProfile
	}
	if secret {
		line += " key 0 " + labcreds.Key
	}
	return line
}

// l73EOSApply starts from the startup config, as l73NXOSApply does.
func l73EOSApply(d l73DeviceTLS) []string {
	out := []string{"aaa group server tacacs+ " + l73Group, "no server " + l73Server, "exit", "no tacacs-server host " + l73Server,
		l73EOSHost(d.Server, true, d.Port, d.SingleConnection, d.Secret)}
	if d.Second != "" {
		out = append(out, l73EOSHost(d.Second, d.SecondTLS, d.SecondPort, false, d.Secret || !d.SecondTLS))
	}
	out = append(out, "aaa group server tacacs+ "+l73Group, "server "+d.Server)
	if d.Second != "" {
		out = append(out, "server "+d.Second)
	}
	return append(out, "exit")
}

// l73EOSEnrol is EOS's enrolment: two exec commands that make the key on
// the device and print the request, the exec copies that move the scp'd
// public certificates from flash: into certificate:, and the SSL profile's
// configuration lines.
func l73EOSEnrol(node string) (keyGen, csr string, load, profile []string) {
	keyGen = "security pki key generate rsa 2048 " + l73CertID + ".key"
	csr = "security pki certificate generate signing-request key " + l73CertID + ".key parameters common-name " + node
	for _, f := range []string{"l73-root.crt", "l73-inter.crt", l73CertID + ".crt"} {
		load = append(load, "copy flash:"+f+" certificate:"+f)
	}
	profile = []string{"management security", "ssl profile " + l73EOSProfile,
		"certificate " + l73CertID + ".crt key " + l73CertID + ".key",
		"chain certificate l73-inter.crt", "trust certificate l73-root.crt", "exit", "exit"}
	return keyGen, csr, load, profile
}

// l73Step is one question a command may ask, and its answer. A reply may
// name {ROOT}, {INTER}, {LEAF} (PEM, ending in a newline), {NODE} and {ADDR}.
type l73Step struct{ Prompt, Reply string }

// l73Dialog is one configuration line and the questions it may ask, each
// answered at most once, in whatever order the device asks them. Done, when
// set, must appear before the configuration prompt returns, or the line did
// not do what it was typed for.
type l73Dialog struct {
	Cmd   string
	Steps []l73Step
	Done  string
}

func l73Lines(lines ...string) []l73Dialog {
	out := make([]l73Dialog, len(lines))
	for i, ln := range lines {
		out[i] = l73Dialog{Cmd: ln}
	}
	return out
}

// l73CiscoEnrol is the documents' terminal enrolment: a key pair made on
// the device, two trustpoints (the lab root, and the device's own, whose CA
// is the lab intermediate), a request read off the screen, the lab CA's
// answer pasted back. Only public certificates are typed.
func l73CiscoEnrol(family, node string) (prep []l73Dialog, enroll, load l73Dialog) {
	switch family {
	case "iosxe":
		paste := func(pem string) []l73Step {
			return []l73Step{{"on a line by itself", pem + "quit"}, {"accept this certificate", "yes"}}
		}
		prep = []l73Dialog{{Cmd: "crypto key generate ec keysize 256 label " + l73CiscoKey, Steps: []l73Step{{"replace them", "yes"}}}}
		prep = append(prep, l73Lines("crypto pki trustpoint "+l73RootProfile, "enrollment terminal", "revocation-check none", "exit",
			"crypto pki trustpoint "+l73CertID, "enrollment terminal pem", "subject-name CN="+node, "serial-number none",
			"ip-address none", "revocation-check none", "eckeypair "+l73CiscoKey, "chain-validation continue "+l73RootProfile,
			"exit")...)
		prep = append(prep,
			l73Dialog{Cmd: "crypto pki authenticate " + l73RootProfile, Steps: paste("{ROOT}"), Done: "successfully imported"},
			l73Dialog{Cmd: "crypto pki authenticate " + l73CertID, Steps: paste("{INTER}"), Done: "successfully imported"})
		enroll = l73Dialog{Cmd: "crypto pki enroll " + l73CertID, Steps: []l73Step{
			{"serial number in the subject name", "no"}, {"IP address in the subject name", "no"},
			{"Display Certificate Request to terminal", "yes"}, {"Redisplay enrollment request", "no"}},
			Done: "END CERTIFICATE REQUEST"}
		load = l73Dialog{Cmd: "crypto pki import " + l73CertID + " certificate",
			Steps: []l73Step{{"on a line by itself", "{LEAF}quit"}}, Done: "successfully imported"}
	case "nxos":
		prep = l73Lines("crypto key generate ecc label "+l73CiscoKey+" exportable modulus 256",
			"crypto ca trustpoint "+l73RootProfile, "revocation-check none", "exit",
			"crypto ca trustpoint "+l73CertID, "ecckeypair "+l73CiscoKey, "revocation-check none", "exit")
		prep = append(prep,
			l73Dialog{Cmd: "crypto ca authenticate " + l73RootProfile,
				Steps: []l73Step{{"END OF INPUT", "{ROOT}END OF INPUT"}, {"accept this certificate", "yes"}}},
			l73Dialog{Cmd: "crypto ca authenticate " + l73CertID,
				Steps: []l73Step{{"END OF INPUT", "{INTER}{ROOT}END OF INPUT"}, {"accept this certificate", "yes"}}})
		enroll = l73Dialog{Cmd: "crypto ca enroll " + l73CertID, Steps: []l73Step{
			{"Password:", ""}, {"serial number in the subject name", "no"}, {"IP address in the subject name", "yes"},
			{"ip address:", "{ADDR}"}, {"Alternate Subject Name ?", "yes"}, {"Enter Alternate Subject Name", "{NODE}"}},
			Done: "END CERTIFICATE REQUEST"}
		load = l73Dialog{Cmd: "crypto ca import " + l73CertID + " certificate",
			Steps: []l73Step{{"END OF INPUT", "{LEAF}END OF INPUT"}}}
	}
	return prep, enroll, load
}

// l73NextAnswer is what the device waits for in tail: the unanswered step
// whose prompt appears first (its index), or the configuration prompt (-1)
// when that appears before any of them. ok is false while neither has.
func l73NextAnswer(tail string, steps []l73Step, used []bool, prompt string) (step int, ok bool) {
	best, at := -1, strings.Index(tail, prompt)
	for n, s := range steps {
		if used[n] {
			continue
		}
		if i := strings.Index(tail, s.Prompt); i >= 0 && (at < 0 || i < at) {
			best, at = n, i
		}
	}
	return best, at >= 0
}

func l73Expand(reply string, vals map[string]string) string {
	for _, k := range []string{"ROOT", "INTER", "LEAF", "NODE", "ADDR"} {
		reply = strings.ReplaceAll(reply, "{"+k+"}", vals[k])
	}
	return reply
}

// l73RefusalMarks are how IOS XE's and NX-OS's parsers, and command
// authorization, refuse a line (iosxeConfigure and nxosConfigure check the
// same).
var l73RefusalMarks = []string{"% Invalid", "% Incomplete", "% Unrecognized", "% Ambiguous", "% Error",
	"authorization failed", "Invalid command", "ERROR:"}

// l73Refusal is the first line of out that refuses what was typed; "" when
// none does.
func l73Refusal(out string) string {
	for _, ln := range strings.Split(strings.ReplaceAll(out, "\r", ""), "\n") {
		for _, m := range l73RefusalMarks {
			if strings.Contains(ln, m) {
				return strings.TrimSpace(ln)
			}
		}
	}
	return ""
}
```

```go
// Junos prefixes an inherited option with "+" and a container with ">";
// the word after the marker is the option. Before this, a client offered as
// "> tls" read as no tls at all.
func TestL71SaysReadsJunosMarkedOptions(t *testing.T) {
	help := "+ apply-groups         Groups\n  secret               Shared secret\n> tls                  TLS configuration\n"
	if line, ok := l71Says(help, []string{"tls"}); !ok || line != "> tls                  TLS configuration" {
		t.Fatalf("%q %v", line, ok)
	}
	if _, ok := l71Says(help, []string{"apply-groups"}); !ok {
		t.Fatal("a + option missed")
	}
	if _, ok := l71Says("> \n", []string{"tls"}); ok {
		t.Fatal("a bare marker read as an option")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Fix `l71Says`.** Replace its body's field check:
```go
func l71Says(help string, yes []string) (string, bool) {
	for _, line := range strings.Split(help, "\n") {
		f := strings.Fields(line)
		if len(f) > 1 && (f[0] == "+" || f[0] == ">") {
			f = f[1:] // Junos marks inherited (+) and container (>) options
		}
		if len(f) == 0 {
			continue
		}
		for _, y := range yes {
			if f[0] == y {
				return strings.TrimSpace(line), true
			}
		}
	}
	return "", false
}
```
Update its doc comment's "the first token of an indented help line" to "the first token of a help line, after a Junos `+` or `>` marker".

- [ ] **Step 5: Extract `l71AskHelp`** (lab tag, no behaviour change). In `tests/lab/l71_test.go`, add after `l71WaitEmptyLine`:
```go
// l71Answer is one help request's outcome: what the CLI printed, the masked
// exchange to commit, whether Ctrl-U left an empty line (if not, nothing
// more may be typed: close the session), and whether the help never
// finished printing.
type l71Answer struct {
	Help, Masked string
	Cleared      bool
	WaitErr      error
}

// l71AskHelp types ask and "?" with no newline, waits for a control word
// and then the configuration prompt redisplayed, then Ctrl-U. Its error is
// an I/O error writing to the CLI.
func l71AskHelp(c *cliConn, ask string, control []string, cfgMark string) (l71Answer, error) {
	from := len(c.transcript())
	if _, err := c.in.Write([]byte(ask + "?")); err != nil {
		return l71Answer{}, err
	}
	tail, werr := c.expectFrom(from, 20*time.Second, control...)
	if werr == nil {
		at := len(tail)
		for _, w := range control {
			if i := strings.Index(tail, w); i >= 0 && i < at {
				at = i
			}
		}
		_, werr = c.expectFrom(from+at, 20*time.Second, cfgMark)
	}
	help := strings.ReplaceAll(c.transcript()[from:], "\r", "")
	if _, err := c.in.Write([]byte{0x15}); err != nil { // Ctrl-U: the typed line is dropped, never entered
		return l71Answer{}, err
	}
	cleared := l71WaitEmptyLine(c, 5*time.Second)
	return l71Answer{Help: help, Masked: l71MaskExchange(c.transcript()[from:]), Cleared: cleared, WaitErr: werr}, nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: `scpWrite`.** Create `tests/lab/l73_test.go`:
```go
//go:build lab

package lab

import (
	"bufio"
	"bytes"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"golang.org/x/crypto/ssh"
)

// scpWrite is the source side of scp's protocol to a sink at path: wait for
// its ack, send the C line, ack, the bytes and a NUL, ack. L73 sends only
// public certificates this way.
func scpWrite(c *ssh.Client, path string, body []byte) error {
	s, err := c.NewSession()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer s.Close()
	defer time.AfterFunc(60*time.Second, func() { s.Close() }).Stop()
	in, _ := s.StdinPipe()
	out, _ := s.StdoutPipe()
	var stderr bytes.Buffer
	s.Stderr = &stderr
	if err := s.Start("scp -t " + path); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	r := bufio.NewReader(out)
	ack := func() error {
		b, err := r.ReadByte()
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("scp: %v %s", err, oneLine(stderr.String()))
		}
		if b != 0 {
			msg, _ := r.ReadString('\n')
			return fmt.Errorf("scp refused: %s %s", strings.TrimSpace(msg), oneLine(stderr.String()))
		}
		return nil
	}
	frame := scpSinkFrame(filepath.Base(path), body)
	nl := bytes.IndexByte(frame, '\n')
	for _, step := range [][]byte{nil, frame[:nl+1], frame[nl+1:]} {
		if step != nil {
			if _, err := in.Write(step); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		if err := ack(); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	in.Close()
	if err := s.Wait(); err != nil && err != io.EOF {
		return fmt.Errorf("scp: %v %s", err, oneLine(stderr.String()))
	}
	return nil
}
```
